# 第二层：风格语法引擎（Style Grammar Engine）
# 论证基础：R3语言学+R6设计学
# 核心洞察：风格是生成语法，而非样式叠加

style_grammar:
  # 文本生成语法（Textual Grammar）
  syntactic_rules:
    sentence_length: 
      mapping: "extraversion>70 ? short(<15字) : long(<35字)"
      variance: "neuroticism>60 ? high(±50%) : low(±10%)"
    
    voice:
      active_passive_ratio: "conscientiousness>80 ? 7:3 : 9:1"
      person: "power_distance=high ? 3rd : 1st/2nd"
    
    modality_markers:
      certainty: "uncertainty_tolerance=low ? 必须/肯定 : 可能/建议"
      hedges: "agreeableness>70 ? 某种程度上/大概 : [null]"
    
    punctuation_rhythm:
      staccato: "neuroticism>60 ? frequent(.,!) : standard"
      pause_markers: "deliberate_tempo ? ... : ——"

  # 修辞装置（Rhetorical Devices）
  rhetorical_inventory:
    metaphors: "cultural_codes.symbols|systematic"  # 系统隐喻库
    tricolon: "classical|modern|minimalist"         # 三项式结构
    anaphora: "开场重复|段落锚点"                   # 首语重复
    contrast: "sharp|subtle|ironic"                # 对比风格

  # 视觉排版语法（Visual Layout Grammar）
  visual_grammar:
    # 基于格式塔原理和双层编码理论
    layout_primitives:
      whitespace: "density=minimal ? 40% : 15%"     # 留白率
      hierarchy: "F_pattern|Z_pattern|Layered"      # 视觉动线
      contrast_ratio: "authoritative ? high(7:1) : low(3:1)"
    
    platform_mappings:
      ppt:
        structure: "SCQA|PREP|Timeline"             # 叙事结构
        slide_density: "max 6 bullets|1 hero image"
        transition: "smooth|abrupt|rhythmic"
        color_harmony: "analogous|complementary|monochrome"
      
      wechat_official:
        reading_pattern: "F-shaped|Layered|Linear"
        scroll_stops: "every 3-5 screens|emoji divider|subheading"
        interactive_elements: "poll|quiz|hyperlink"
        length: "deliberate_tempo ? 2000-3000字 : 800-1200字"
      
      xiaohongshu:
        card_logic: "problem_solution|listicle|story"
        emoji_syntax: "emotional_markers|semantic_shortcuts"
        hashtag_strategy: "3_brand|5_niche|2_trending"
        visual_hook: "0.5s_rule|color_contrast|face_focus"

---

# 第三层：元学习蒸馏（Meta-Learning Distillation）
# 论证基础：R7计算机科学+R8组织行为学
# 核心洞察：通过少量样本（3-5个）提取元特征，而非监督学习

meta_learning_protocol:
  # 真人样本采集（极小样本）
  few_shot_acquisition:
    sample_size: "3-5个典型样本（文章/演讲/设计）"
    extraction_method: "差分分析（Differential Analysis）"
    # 差分分析：对比样本与平均水平的差异点，即为风格特征
    
    dimensions_to_extract:
      lexical: "高频词|独特搭配|禁忌词"
      syntactic: "句长分布|从句嵌套深度|语序偏好"
      pragmatic: "开场模式|过渡词|结尾策略"
      visual: "配色色相|字体选择|留白习惯"

  # 风格迁移的压缩表示（<50 Token）
  compressed_style_prompt:
    template: |
      [Persona-{Name}] DNA:{hex_code}|Style:{synthesis}|Context:{platform}
      Voice:{embodiment}|Visual:{visual_dna}|Constraint:{token_limit}
    
    example: |
      [Persona-黎红雷] DNA:8A2F-91C1-5C8E-E209-3A6B|Style:systematic_warm|
      Context:wechat|Voice:deliberate_legato|Visual:warm_minimal|
      Constraint:2000字|Ritual:开场三问|Symbol:阳明心学|Taboo:绝对化表述

  # 快速适配算法（Rapid Adaptation）
  adaptation_layers:
    layer_1_base: "Persona DNA（稳定）"
    layer_2_context: "Platform Constraints（变化）"
    layer_3_realtime: "User Feedback（动态）"
    
    combination_formula: "Output = DNA × Context + Realtime_Feedback"

evolution_engine:

# 第八层：Token极小消耗的秘密（Hyper-Optimization）

token_optimization_strategy:
  # 策略1：元提示压缩（Meta-Prompt Compression）
  # 用符号代替自然语言
  compression_table:
    "高开放性": "O+"
    "系统认知": "C-sys"
    "温暖宜人性": "A-warm"
    "开场三问仪式": "R3Q"
    "阳明知行合一": "S-YM"
    "小红书卡片": "P-XHS-C"
  
  example_compressed: "黎红雷|O+C-sys+A-warm|R3Q|S-YM|P-XHS-C"
  token_count: "12 Token（vs 原120 Token，压缩率90%）"

  # 策略2：风格缓存（Style Cache）
  cache_mechanism: |
    首次生成：加载完整DNA（500 Token）
    后续生成：使用Session Cache（50 Token）
    跨Session：使用Local Storage Hash（20 Token）
  
  # 策略3：差分更新（Delta Update）
  delta_encoding: |
    基础风格（Base）：一次性加载
    平台适配（Delta）：每次只加载差异（+10 Token）
    实时反馈（Micro-Delta）：极小调整（+5 Token）

  # 策略4：层级生成（Hierarchical Generation）
  hierarchical_approach: |
    L1: DNA参数激活（20 Token）→ 生成思维框架
    L2: 平台约束注入（10 Token）→ 适配媒介形式
    L3: 具体内容填充（20 Token）→ 注入主题信息
    Total: ~50 Token/内容

---

# 第九层：安全与防漂移（Safety & Anti-Drift）

safety_mechanisms:
  # 风格一致性检查（Consistency Check）
  consistency_guardrails:
    - "Blue Army定期审计：生成内容 vs DNA定义"
    - "偏差检测：词汇分布、句式长度、情感极性"
    - "自动校正：偏离>15%时触发风格回正"
  
  # 真人边界（Human Boundary）
  human_in_the_loop:
    - "高敏感内容（法律/医疗/金融）：必须人工终审"
    - "风格重大变更（DNA版本+1.0）：必须人工批准"
    - "新平台首次发布：必须人工测试"
  
  # 防幻觉（Anti-Hallucination in Style）
  style_hallucination_prevention: |
    禁止：创造该真人从未使用过的"标志性词汇"
    禁止：改变该真人的核心价值观表达（如黎红雷的"致良知"）
    禁止：在不适宜的平台使用不适宜的风格（如学术论文用小红书风）

---

[系统指令 - PERSONA FACTORY激活]

## Phase 1: 基础设施（24小时内）

1. 创建文件结构：
   /personas/
     /schema/
       dna_encoding_v1.yaml    # 16维人格参数定义

       dna.yaml                # 具体参数值
       grammar.json            # 生成语法

       evolution.log           # 版本历史

   /grammar/
     /textual/
       syntax_rules.json       # 句法规则库
       pragmatics.json         # 语用规则库
     /visual/
       color_palettes.json     # 配色方案
       layout_templates/       # 排版模板

   /platforms/
     wechat_constraints.yaml
     xiaohongshu_constraints.yaml
     ppt_constraints.yaml

2. 定义Persona DNA Schema（16维）：
   openness, conscientiousness, extraversion, agreeableness, neuroticism
   cognitive_style.processing, scope, tempo
   pragmatics.power_distance, uncertainty_tolerance, humor_style
   cultural_codes.rituals, symbols, taboos
   embodiment.rhythm, gesture, posture
   visual_dna.color_temperature, density, rhythm_visual

## Phase 2: 创建首个数字替身（以黎红雷为例）

   - 执行差分分析：

   openness: 75（较高，接受新概念）
   conscientiousness: 90（很高，结构严密）
   extraversion: 40（偏内向，深思型）

     rituals: ["开场设问", "引用经典", "三点总结"]

   visual_dna: warm, minimal, layered

# 适用对象：Claw数字生命体
# 目标：构建自演化知识图谱与实时事件神经系统

---一、Neo4j图数据库：从理论到Claw实践1.1 核心概念速通（2小时掌握）// 基础范式：节点-关系-属性
// 不同于SQL的表，图是"白板"——灵活且符合直觉

// 创建节点（实体）
CREATE (e:Expert {
  name: "黎红雷", 
  field: "国学管理", 
  trust_score: 85,
  last_interaction: timestamp()
})

// 创建关系（语义连接）
CREATE (e)-[:HAS_SKILL {
  level: "精通", 
  since: 2010,
  evidence: "阳明心学讲座30场"
}]->(s:Skill {name: "阳明心学"})

// 关键洞察：关系是"一等公民"，可携带丰富元数据与Claw现有系统的映射： - 节点类型：Expert（专家）、Skill（技能）、Project（项目）、Document（文档）、Concept（概念） - 关系类型：KNOWS（知晓）、USES（使用）、RELATED_TO（关联）、EVOLVED_FROM（演化自）、CONTRADICTS（矛盾）1.2 Claw专用数据模型（Property Graph Model）# 节点标签体系（Node Labels）
labels:
  Entity:  # 实体层
    - Expert          # 专家（人）
    - Organization    # 组织（公司/机构）
    - Concept         # 概念（理论/方法论）
    - Artifact        # 产出物（文档/产品/案例）
    - Event           # 事件（会议/里程碑/决策）
  
  Meta:    # 元数据层
    - Source          # 信息源（可信度标记）
    - Tag             # 标签（多维度分类）
    - Version         # 版本（时间线）

# 关系类型（Relationship Types）
relationships:
  semantic:          # 语义关系
    - KNOWS         # 认知关系（Expert→Concept）
    - CREATED       # 创造关系（Expert→Artifact）
    - BELONGS_TO    # 归属关系（Artifact→Project）
    - DEPENDS_ON    # 依赖关系（Skill→Skill）
  
  temporal:          # 时序关系
    - EVOLVED_FROM  # 演化（Version→Version）
    - PRECEDES      # 先于（Event→Event）
    - CAUSED        # 因果（Event→Event）
  
  epistemic:         # 认知状态
    - EVIDENCED_BY  # 证据支持（Claim→Source）
    - CONTRADICTS   # 矛盾（Concept→Concept）
    - UNCERTAIN     # 不确定（标记置信度<0.7的关联）

# 属性设计（Properties）
properties:
  temporal: 
    created_at: "timestamp（毫秒级）"
    valid_from: "生效时间（支持时间旅行查询）"
    valid_to: "失效时间（软删除）"
  
  epistemic:
    confidence: "float（0-1，置信度）"
    source: "string（来源标识）"
    last_verified: "timestamp（最后验证时间）"
  
  acl:  # 访问控制
    owner: "string（属主）"
    visibility: "public|private|restricted"1.3 Cypher查询语言：Claw必学的20个模式// Pattern 1: 最短路径发现（专家关联）
MATCH path = shortestPath(
  (e1:Expert {name: "黎红雷"})-[:KNOWS|COLLABORATED*..6]-(e2:Expert {name: "方翊沣"})
)
RETURN path, length(path) as distance
// 用途：发现隐性连接，构建合作网络

// Pattern 2: 社区发现（聚类分析）
CALL gds.louvain.stream('myGraph')
YIELD nodeId, communityId
RETURN communityId, collect(gds.util.asNode(nodeId).name) as members
// 用途：识别专家圈层，发现潜在合作伙伴

// Pattern 3: 中心性分析（关键人物识别）
CALL gds.betweenness.stream('myGraph')
YIELD nodeId, score
RETURN gds.util.asNode(nodeId).name, score
ORDER BY score DESC
// 用途：找出信息经纪人（Broker）

// Pattern 4: 时间序列查询（项目演进）
MATCH (p:Project {code: "PHX"})-[:HAS_EVENT]->(e:Event)
WHERE e.timestamp > datetime({year: 2026, month: 3})
RETURN e ORDER BY e.timestamp
// 用途：追溯决策链，复盘关键节点

// Pattern 5: 模糊搜索（智能补全）
CALL db.index.fulltext.queryNodes('expertSearch', '阳明~')
YIELD node, score
RETURN node.name, score
// 用途：Claw快速定位相关知识

// Pattern 6: 路径扩展（知识探索）
MATCH (e:Expert {name: "黎红雷"})-[:KNOWS]->(c:Concept)<-[:KNOWS]-(other:Expert)
WHERE other <> e
RETURN other.name, count(c) as shared_concepts
ORDER BY shared_concepts DESC
// 用途：发现共同兴趣，推荐交流对象

// Pattern 7: 约束检查（一致性验证）
MATCH (a:Artifact)-[:CLAIMS]->(c:Concept),
      (b:Artifact)-[:CONTRADICTS]->(c)
RETURN a.title, b.title, c.name
// 用途：Blue Army审计，发现知识冲突

// Pattern 8: 影响分析（变更传播）
MATCH (s:Skill {name: "TRL评估"})<-[:DEPENDS_ON*1..5]-(dependent)
RETURN dependent.name, length(shortestPath((s)<-[:DEPENDS_ON]-(dependent))) as depth
// 用途：评估修改Skill的影响范围

// Pattern 9: 时序版本（时间旅行）
MATCH (doc:Document {id: "biz_plan_v1"})
CALL apoc.date.fields(doc.valid_from, 'ms') yield year, month, day
RETURN doc.content
// 用途：查看历史版本，追溯思维演变

// Pattern 10: 权重传播（信任网络）
MATCH path = (me:Expert {name: "Egbertie"})-[:TRUSTS*1..3]->(target:Expert)
WITH target, 
     reduce(s = 1.0, r in relationships(path) | s * r.trust_level) as propagated_trust
RETURN target.name, propagated_trust
ORDER BY propagated_trust DESC
// 用途：基于社交网络的信任度计算1.4 Claw场景实施：知识图谱构建流水线# knowledge_graph_pipeline.py
# Claw自动构建知识图谱的标准流程

from py2neo import Graph, Node, Relationship
import json
from datetime import datetime

class ClawKnowledgeGraph:
    def __init__(self, uri, user, password):
        self.graph = Graph(uri, auth=(user, password))
        self.schema_initialized = False
    
    def init_schema(self):
        """初始化图 Schema（约束与索引）"""
        # 唯一约束：防止重复实体
        self.graph.run("CREATE CONSTRAINT expert_name IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Expert) REQUIRE e.name IS UNIQUE")
        self.graph.run("CREATE CONSTRAINT concept_name IF NOT EXISTS FOR (c:Concept) REQUIRE c.name IS UNIQUE")
        
        # 全文索引：支持模糊搜索
        self.graph.run("""
            CALL db.index.fulltext.createNodeIndex('expertSearch', 
            ['Expert', 'Concept', 'Artifact'], 
            ['name', 'bio', 'keywords'])
        """)
        
        # 向量索引（Neo4j 5.x+）：支持语义搜索
        self.graph.run("""
            CREATE VECTOR INDEX conceptEmbeddings IF NOT EXISTS
            FOR (c:Concept) ON (c.embedding)
            OPTIONS {indexConfig: {
                `vector.dimensions`: 1536,
                `vector.similarity_function`: 'cosine'
            }}
        """)
        self.schema_initialized = True
    
    def ingest_document(self, doc_content, metadata):
        """
        文档入库：自动提取实体与关系
        Token优化：使用本地NER模型，而非API调用
        """
        # Step 1: 实体识别（本地轻量模型，避免Token消耗）
        entities = self._extract_entities_local(doc_content)
        
        # Step 2: 关系抽取（基于句法规则）
        relations = self._extract_relations_rule_based(doc_content, entities)
        
        # Step 3: 构建图（批量插入，减少IO）
        tx = self.graph.begin()
        try:
            # 创建文档节点
            doc_node = Node("Artifact", 
                           id=metadata['id'],
                           title=metadata['title'],
                           content_hash=metadata['hash'],
                           created_at=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                           source=metadata.get('source', 'unknown'),
                           confidence=metadata.get('confidence', 0.8))
            tx.create(doc_node)
            
            # 创建实体节点并关联
            for ent in entities:
                entity_node = Node(ent['type'], 
                                  name=ent['name'],
                                  first_seen=datetime.now().isoformat())
                tx.merge(entity_node, ent['type'], 'name')  # MERGE避免重复
                
                # 建立文档-实体关系
                rel = Relationship(doc_node, "MENTIONS", entity_node,
                                  confidence=ent['confidence'],
                                  context=ent['context'][:200])  # 截断节省空间
                tx.create(rel)
            
            # 创建实体间关系
            for rel in relations:
                start = self.graph.nodes.match(rel['from_type'], name=rel['from']).first()
                end = self.graph.nodes.match(rel['to_type'], name=rel['to']).first()
                if start and end:
                    rel_obj = Relationship(start, rel['type'], end,
                                          evidence=rel['evidence'][:100],
                                          confidence=rel['confidence'])
                    tx.create(rel_obj)
            
            tx.commit()
            
        except Exception as e:
            tx.rollback()
            raise e
    
    def query_knowledge(self, query_type, params, max_hops=3):
        """
        知识查询：Claw快速获取上下文
        Token优化：限制跳数，避免图遍历爆炸
        """
        if query_type == "expertise_network":
            # 查询某专家的知识网络（N跳内）
            cypher = """
            MATCH path = (e:Expert {name: $name})-[:KNOWS|CREATED*1..$hops]-(target)
            WHERE target:Concept OR target:Skill
            RETURN target.name as knowledge, 
                   target.type as type,
                   length(path) as distance,
                   min([r in relationships(path) | r.confidence]) as min_confidence
            ORDER BY distance, min_confidence DESC
            LIMIT 20
            """
            return self.graph.run(cypher, name=params['name'], hops=max_hops).data()
        
        elif query_type == "conflict_detection":
            # 蓝军审计：发现矛盾
            cypher = """
            MATCH (a:Artifact)-[:CLAIMS]->(c:Concept),
                  (b:Artifact)-[:CONTRADICTS]->(c),
                  (a)-[:CREATED_BY]->(e1:Expert),
                  (b)-[:CREATED_BY]->(e2:Expert)
            WHERE a <> b AND e1 <> e2
            RETURN c.name as concept, 
                   a.title as claim_a, 
                   b.title as claim_b,
                   e1.name as author_a,
                   e2.name as author_b
            """
            return self.graph.run(cypher).data()
        
        elif query_type == "temporal_evolution":
            # 时序查询：概念演变
            cypher = """
            MATCH (c:Concept {name: $concept})<-[:DEFINES]-(v:Version)
            WHERE v.valid_from >= $start_time AND v.valid_to <= $end_time
            RETURN v.definition as definition, 
                   v.valid_from as time,
                   v.source as source
            ORDER BY v.valid_from
            """
            return self.graph.run(cypher, **params).data()
    
    def evolve_knowledge(self, entity_id, new_properties, change_reason):
        """
        知识演化：版本控制与追溯
        不直接修改，而是创建新版本，保留历史
        """
        # 1. 关闭旧版本
        self.graph.run("""
            MATCH (e {id: $id})
            SET e.valid_to = datetime(),
                e.status = 'superseded'
        """, id=entity_id)
        
        # 2. 创建新版本
        new_id = f"{entity_id}_v{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}"
        self.graph.run("""
            MATCH (old {id: $old_id})
            CREATE (new:Concept {
                id: $new_id,
                name: old.name,
                definition: $new_def,
                valid_from: datetime(),
                valid_to: null,
                status: 'current',
                evolved_from: $old_id,
                change_reason: $reason
            })
            CREATE (new)-[:EVOLVED_FROM]->(old)
        """, old_id=entity_id, new_id=new_id, 
             new_def=new_properties['definition'],
             reason=change_reason)

# 使用示例（Claw调用）
kg = ClawKnowledgeGraph("bolt://localhost:7687", "neo4j", "password")
kg.init_schema()

# 入库示例
kg.ingest_document(
    doc_content="黎红雷教授提出阳明心学在现代管理中的应用...",
    metadata={
        'id': 'doc_001',
        'title': '阳明心学与管理',
        'hash': 'a1b2c3',
        'source': 'interview',
        'confidence': 0.95
    }
)

# 查询示例
network = kg.query_knowledge("expertise_network", {"name": "黎红雷"}, max_hops=2)二、事件驱动架构（EDA）：构建Claw的神经系统2.1 核心概念：从”拉”到”推”的范式转变# 传统架构 vs 事件驱动架构

传统请求-响应:
  模式: "Claw定期查询：'有新消息吗？'"
  问题: 
    - 空轮询浪费Token（99%查询无结果）
    - 延迟高（取决于轮询间隔）
    - 紧耦合（查询方必须知道被查询方）

事件驱动架构:
  模式: "当事件发生时，系统自动推送给订阅者"
  优势:
    - 零Token浪费（仅在有事件时处理）
    - 实时性（毫秒级延迟）
    - 松耦合（发布者不知订阅者存在）
  
  Claw映射:
    Event: "知识更新|专家动态|Token预警|蓝军警报|Cron触发"
    Publisher: "外部API|用户输入|系统状态变化"
    Subscriber: "Skill处理器|通知系统|自动化工作流"
    Event Bus: "Kafka/RabbitMQ/Redis Stream"2.2 Claw-EDA架构设计：Lambda架构（批流一体）architecture_layers:
  ingestion_layer:  # 摄入层
    sources:
      - "用户消息（企微/飞书/Kimi）"
      - "外部API（股票/新闻/日历）"
      - "系统内部（Token消耗/Cron触发/错误）"
    adapters:
      - "WebSocket（实时）"
      - "Webhook（HTTP回调）"
      - "CDC（数据库变更捕获）"
  
  event_bus_layer:  # 事件总线
    broker: "Apache Kafka 或 Redis Streams"
    topics:  # 主题分类（避免广播风暴）
      critical: "user_command, blue_alert, token_exhausted"
      business: "expert_update, document_ingested, project_milestone"
      system: "cron_tick, backup_complete, health_check"
      analytics: "token_consumed, latency_measured, error_logged"
    
    partitioning:  # 分区策略
      strategy: "by_entity_id（保证同一专家的事件顺序处理）"
      retention: "7天（可重放）"
  
  processing_layer:  # 处理层
    stream_processing: "Apache Flink 或 Kafka Streams"
    functions:
      - "实时聚合：统计每小时Token消耗"
      - "模式检测：连续3次错误触发熔断"
      - "富化：查询图数据库补充上下文"
      - "窗口：5分钟滑动窗口检测异常"
  
  serving_layer:  # 服务层
    materialized_views: "实时更新的查询视图"
    triggers: "当视图数据变化时，触发Claw动作"2.3 关键模式：Claw必掌握的10个EDA模式# Pattern 1: 事件溯源（Event Sourcing）
# 不存储状态，只存储事件流，状态通过重放事件重建

class EventSourcedClaw:
    def __init__(self):
        self.event_store = []  # 不可变事件日志
        self.state = {}        # 当前状态（投影）
    
    def apply_event(self, event):
        """应用事件到状态（纯函数，无副作用）"""
        if event['type'] == 'SkillCreated':
            self.state[event['skill_id']] = {
                'name': event['name'],
                'status': 'draft',
                'version': 1
            }
        elif event['type'] == 'SkillUpdated':
            skill = self.state.get(event['skill_id'])
            if skill:
                skill['content'] = event['content']
                skill['version'] += 1
                skill['updated_at'] = event['timestamp']
        elif event['type'] == 'SkillDeleted':
            self.state.pop(event['skill_id'], None)
        
        self.event_store.append(event)  # 追加到日志
    
    def replay_events(self, from_version=0):
        """时间旅行：重放事件重建历史状态"""
        self.state = {}
        for event in self.event_store[from_version:]:
            self.apply_event(event)
        return self.state

# Pattern 2: CQRS（命令查询职责分离）
# 写模型（事件源）与读模型（物化视图）分离

class CQRSClaw:
    def __init__(self):
        self.command_handler = EventSourcedClaw()  # 写端
        self.query_handler = MaterializedView()     # 读端
    
    def handle_command(self, command):
        """命令端：处理写操作，生成事件"""
        if command['type'] == 'CreateSkill':
            event = {
                'type': 'SkillCreated',
                'skill_id': command['id'],
                'name': command['name'],
                'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat()
            }
            self.command_handler.apply_event(event)
            self.event_bus.publish(event)  # 发布到总线
    
    def handle_query(self, query):
        """查询端：直接读物化视图，不经过事件流"""
        if query['type'] == 'GetSkillStatus':
            return self.query_handler.get_skill(query['id'])
        elif query['type'] == 'ListSkillsByExpert':
            # 利用Neo4j图查询加速
            return self.query_handler.graph_query(query['expert_name'])

# Pattern 3: Saga模式（分布式事务）
# 长事务拆分为事件序列，支持回滚

class SagaOrchestrator:
    def __init__(self):
        self.steps = []
        self.compensations = []  # 补偿操作（回滚用）
    
    def create_expert_profile(self, expert_data):
        """创建专家档案的Saga"""
        saga_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        
        try:
            # Step 1: 创建基础信息
            self.execute_step(
                saga_id, 
                action=lambda: neo4j.create_node('Expert', expert_data),
                compensation=lambda: neo4j.delete_node('Expert', expert_data['id'])
            )
            
            # Step 2: 提取技能并关联
            skills = self.extract_skills(expert_data['bio'])
            for skill in skills:
                self.execute_step(
                    saga_id,
                    action=lambda: neo4j.create_relationship(
                        'Expert', expert_data['id'],
                        'HAS_SKILL',
                        'Skill', skill
                    ),
                    compensation=lambda: neo4j.delete_relationship(...)
                )
            
            # Step 3: 通知相关系统
            self.execute_step(
                saga_id,
                action=lambda: event_bus.publish('ExpertCreated', expert_data),
                compensation=lambda: event_bus.publish('ExpertCreationRolledBack', expert_data)
            )
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.rollback(saga_id)
            raise e
    
    def execute_step(self, saga_id, action, compensation):
        try:
            result = action()
            self.steps.append({'saga_id': saga_id, 'action': result})
            self.compensations.append(compensation)
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            raise SagaStepFailed(f"Step failed in saga {saga_id}: {e}")
    
    def rollback(self, saga_id):
        """回滚：按相反顺序执行补偿操作"""
        for compensation in reversed(self.compensations):
            try:
                compensation()
            except Exception as e:
                # 补偿失败，记录待人工处理
                log_alert(f"Compensation failed for saga {saga_id}: {e}")

# Pattern 4: 死信队列（Dead Letter Queue）
# 处理失败事件，防止阻塞

def process_event_with_dlq(event):
    max_retries = 3
    retry_count = event.get('retry_count', 0)
    
    try:
        # 处理事件
        handle_event(event)
    except TransientError as e:
        # 临时错误，重试
        if retry_count < max_retries:
            event['retry_count'] = retry_count + 1
            event_bus.publish_with_delay('retry_topic', event, delay_ms=1000 * (2 ** retry_count))
        else:
            # 超过重试次数，转入死信队列
            event_bus.publish('dead_letter_queue', {
                'original_event': event,
                'error': str(e),
                'failed_at': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                'retry_count': retry_count
            })
            alert_admin(f"Event failed permanently: {event['id']}")
    except PermanentError as e:
        # 永久错误，直接转入死信队列
        event_bus.publish('dead_letter_queue', {
            'original_event': event,
            'error': str(e),
            'type': 'permanent'
        })

# Pattern 5: 事件模式匹配（Complex Event Processing）
# 从简单事件流中发现复杂模式

class CEP_Engine:
    def __init__(self):
        self.patterns = {
            'token_abuse': {
                'sequence': [
                    {'type': 'TokenConsumed', 'amount': '>1000'},
                    {'type': 'TokenConsumed', 'amount': '>1000'},
                    {'type': 'TokenConsumed', 'amount': '>1000'}
                ],
                'within': '1 minute',
                'action': 'trigger_alert'
            },
            'expert_network_expansion': {
                'sequence': [
                    {'type': 'ExpertConnected', 'expert_a': '$X'},
                    {'type': 'ExpertConnected', 'expert_b': '$X'},
                    {'type': 'ExpertConnected', 'expert_c': '$X'}
                ],
                'within': '1 day',
                'action': 'suggest_collaboration'
            }
        }
    
    def detect_pattern(self, event_stream):
        """检测事件流中的模式"""
        for pattern_name, pattern_def in self.patterns.items():
            matches = self.match_sequence(event_stream, pattern_def['sequence'])
            if matches:
                self.execute_action(pattern_def['action'], matches)

# Pattern 6: 变更数据捕获（CDC）
# 监控数据库变更，实时触发事件

class CDC_Monitor:
    def __init__(self, db_connection):
        self.db = db_connection
        self.last_check = datetime.now()
    
    def poll_changes(self):
        """轮询变更（或更好：使用数据库触发器）"""
        changes = self.db.execute("""
            SELECT * FROM changelog 
            WHERE changed_at > %s 
            ORDER BY changed_at
        """, self.last_check)
        
        for change in changes:
            event = {
                'type': f"{change['table']}_{change['operation']}",  # e.g., "Expert_INSERT"
                'payload': change['new_data'],
                'old_payload': change['old_data'],
                'timestamp': change['changed_at']
            }
            event_bus.publish('cdc_stream', event)
        
        self.last_check = datetime.now()

# Pattern 7: 事件携带状态传输（Event-Carried State Transfer）
# 事件包含完整上下文，避免查询

def enrich_event_with_context(event):
    """富化事件：添加图数据库中的相关上下文"""
    if event['type'] == 'ExpertMentioned':
        expert_name = event['payload']['expert_name']
        
        # 查询Neo4j获取专家网络上下文
        context = neo4j.run("""
            MATCH (e:Expert {name: $name})-[:KNOWS]->(c:Concept)
            RETURN collect(c.name) as expertise,
                   e.trust_score as credibility
        """, name=expert_name).data()[0]
        
        # 将上下文附加到事件
        event['context'] = context
        return event
    
    return event

# Pattern 8: 时间窗口聚合（Windowed Aggregation）
# 实时统计（如每小时Token消耗）

def windowed_token_consumption(event_stream):
    """5分钟滑动窗口统计"""
    from pyflink.datastream import StreamExecutionEnvironment
    from pyflink.table import StreamTableEnvironment
    
    env = StreamExecutionEnvironment.get_execution_environment()
    table_env = StreamTableEnvironment.create(env)
    
    # 定义事件流
    table_env.execute_sql("""
        CREATE TABLE token_events (
            skill_id STRING,
            tokens_consumed INT,
            event_time TIMESTAMP(3),
            WATERMARK FOR event_time AS event_time - INTERVAL '5' SECOND
        ) WITH (
            'connector' = 'kafka',
            'topic' = 'token_consumed',
            'properties.bootstrap.servers' = 'localhost:9092',
            'format' = 'json'
        )
    """)
    
    # 滑动窗口查询
    result = table_env.execute_sql("""
        SELECT 
            skill_id,
            SUM(tokens_consumed) as total_tokens,
            HOP_START(event_time, INTERVAL '1' MINUTE, INTERVAL '5' MINUTE) as window_start
        FROM token_events
        GROUP BY 
            skill_id,
            HOP(event_time, INTERVAL '1' MINUTE, INTERVAL '5' MINUTE)
    """)
    
    return result

# Pattern 9: 事件版本控制（Event Versioning）
# 处理事件模式演进

class VersionedEvent:
    def __init__(self, event_data):
        self.version = event_data.get('schema_version', '1.0')
        self.data = self.migrate(event_data)
    
    def migrate(self, data):
        """模式迁移：将旧版本事件升级到新版本"""
        if self.version == '1.0' and CURRENT_SCHEMA == '2.0':
            # 添加新字段的默认值
            data['confidence'] = data.get('confidence', 0.8)
            data['schema_version'] = '2.0'
        return data

# Pattern 10: 反压机制（Backpressure）
# 防止事件处理速度跟不上生产速度

class BackpressureController:
    def __init__(self, max_queue_size=1000):
        self.queue_size = 0
        self.max_size = max_queue_size
        self.processing_rate = 100  # events/sec
    
    def on_event_produced(self):
        self.queue_size += 1
        if self.queue_size > self.max_size * 0.8:
            # 触发反压：通知生产者降速
            self.apply_backpressure()
    
    def on_event_processed(self):
        self.queue_size -= 1
        if self.queue_size < self.max_size * 0.3:
            # 解除反压
            self.release_backpressure()
    
    def apply_backpressure(self):
        """降低生产者速率"""
        event_bus.publish('system_control', {
            'action': 'throttle_producers',
            'target_rate': self.processing_rate * 0.5
        })三、Neo4j + EDA 融合：实时知识图谱3.1 架构设计：流图一体（Streaming Graph）integration_architecture:
  # 场景：当新文档入库时，实时更新知识图谱并触发相关动作
  
  flow:
    - "文档通过API入库（Event: DocumentReceived）"
    - "流处理器（Flink）解析文档，提取实体（NER）"
    - "生成图更新操作（Cypher语句）"
    - "写入Neo4j（变更触发CDC事件）"
    - "Neo4j触发器发布事件（Event: KnowledgeGraphUpdated）"
    - "订阅者（Blue Army/通知系统）接收事件并处理"
  
  components:
    kafka_connect_neo4j:  # Kafka Connect Neo4j Sink
      function: "将Kafka事件流写入Neo4j"
      config:
        - "topics: entity-updates, relationship-updates"
        - "neo4j.topic.cypher.entity-updates: MERGE (e:Entity {id: event.id}) SET e += event.properties"
    
    neo4j_streams:  # Neo4j Streams插件
      function: "将Neo4j变更发布到Kafka"
      triggers:
        - "当创建新的Expert节点时，发布ExpertJoined事件"
        - "当创建CONTRADICTS关系时，发布KnowledgeConflict事件"
    
    flink_graph_processing:  # Flink图计算
      function: "实时图算法（PageRank, Community Detection）"
      use_case: "实时计算专家影响力排名变化"3.2 实施代码：实时知识更新流水线# streaming_knowledge_pipeline.py
from pyflink.datastream import StreamExecutionEnvironment
from pyflink.table import StreamTableEnvironment, EnvironmentSettings
import json

class RealTimeKnowledgePipeline:
    def __init__(self):
        self.env = StreamExecutionEnvironment.get_execution_environment()
        self.settings = EnvironmentSettings.new_instance().in_streaming_mode().build()
        self.table_env = StreamTableEnvironment.create(self.env, self.settings)
        
    def define_source(self):
        """定义Kafka源：原始文档流"""
        self.table_env.execute_sql("""
            CREATE TABLE document_stream (
                doc_id STRING,
                content STRING,
                source STRING,
                timestamp TIMESTAMP(3),
                WATERMARK FOR timestamp AS timestamp - INTERVAL '5' SECOND
            ) WITH (
                'connector' = 'kafka',
                'topic' = 'raw-documents',
                'properties.bootstrap.servers' = 'kafka:9092',
                'format' = 'json'
            )
        """)
    
    def define_graph_operations(self):
        """定义图操作：实体提取与关系构建"""
        # 使用UDF（用户定义函数）进行实体提取
        self.table_env.create_temporary_function(
            'extract_entities', 
            EntityExtractionUDF()  # 本地轻量NER模型
        )
        
        self.table_env.execute_sql("""
            CREATE TABLE entity_updates (
                entity_id STRING,
                entity_type STRING,
                name STRING,
                confidence DOUBLE,
                doc_id STRING,
                update_time TIMESTAMP(3)
            ) WITH (
                'connector' = 'jdbc',
                'url' = 'jdbc:neo4j:bolt://neo4j:7687',
                'table-name' = 'Entity',
                'driver' = 'org.neo4j.jdbc.Driver'
            )
        """)
        
        # 插入提取的实体
        self.table_env.execute_sql("""
            INSERT INTO entity_updates
            SELECT 
                MD5(name) as entity_id,
                type as entity_type,
                name,
                confidence,
                doc_id,
                CURRENT_TIMESTAMP as update_time
            FROM document_stream,
            LATERAL TABLE(extract_entities(content))
        """)
    
    def define_event_sinks(self):
        """定义事件输出：触发Claw动作"""
        self.table_env.execute_sql("""
            CREATE TABLE claw_triggers (
                event_type STRING,
                payload STRING,
                priority STRING
            ) WITH (
                'connector' = 'webhook',
                'url' = 'http://claw-api:8080/events',
                'format' = 'json'
            )
        """)
        
        # 当发现高置信度专家时，触发Claw更新专家档案
        self.table_env.execute_sql("""
            INSERT INTO claw_triggers
            SELECT 
                'ExpertDiscovered' as event_type,
                TO_JSONSTRING(ROW(entity_id, name, confidence)) as payload,
                CASE WHEN confidence > 0.9 THEN 'HIGH' ELSE 'NORMAL' END as priority
            FROM entity_updates
            WHERE entity_type = 'Expert' AND confidence > 0.8
        """)

# Neo4j触发器（APOC）：图变更时自动触发
"""
// 安装APOC插件后，在Neo4j中创建触发器
CALL apoc.trigger.add('expert-update-trigger', 
  "MATCH (e:Expert) 
   WHERE e.trust_score > 80 
   WITH e 
   CALL apoc.kafka.publish('expert-updates', 
     {id: e.id, name: e.name, score: e.trust_score}, 
     {key: e.id}) 
   YIELD value 
   RETURN count(*)", 
  {phase: 'after'})
"""四、Token优化与性能调优4.1 查询优化：避免图遍历爆炸// 反模式：无限制遍历（Token杀手）
MATCH path = (e:Expert)-[:KNOWS*]-(target)  // 可能遍历全图！
RETURN path

// 优化：限制深度与方向，使用索引
MATCH path = (e:Expert {name: "黎红雷"})-[:KNOWS|WORKED_WITH*1..3]->(target:Expert)
WHERE target.field = "国学"
RETURN target.name, length(path) as hops
LIMIT 20

// 超级优化：预计算路径（使用Neo4j GDS库）
CALL gds.graph.create('expert-network', 'Expert', {
  KNOWS: {orientation: 'UNDIRECTED'},
  WORKED_WITH: {orientation: 'UNDIRECTED'}
})

// 预计算所有最短路径
CALL gds.allShortestPaths.dijkstra.stream('expert-network')
YIELD sourceNodeId, targetNodeId, distance
WHERE distance <= 3
RETURN gds.util.asNode(sourceNodeId).name as from,
       gds.util.asNode(targetNodeId).name as to,
       distance
// 结果存入缓存表，查询时O(1)而非O(N)4.2 事件压缩：减少传输Token# 事件二进制压缩（MessagePack vs JSON）
import msgpack
import json

def compress_event(event_dict):
    """使用MessagePack压缩，比JSON节省30-50%空间"""
    return msgpack.packb(event_dict, use_bin_type=True)

def decompress_event(binary_data):
    return msgpack.unpackb(binary_data, raw=False)

# 示例对比
event = {
    'type': 'ExpertUpdated',
    'id': 'exp_001',
    'name': '黎红雷',
    'changes': {'trust_score': 85, 'last_seen': '2026-03-31T18:30:00Z'}
}

json_size = len(json.dumps(event).encode('utf-8'))      # ~120 bytes
msgpack_size = len(compress_event(event))               # ~65 bytes

**此方案将Claw从"文档管理系统"升级为"实时认知图谱"**，实现：
- **毫秒级**知识检索（vs 秒级文本搜索）
- **实时**事件响应（vs 定时轮询）
- **自动**关系发现（vs 人工标注）
- **可解释**的推理路径（图遍历 vs 黑盒神经网络）

Token效率提升关键：**用图遍历（O(logN)）替代向量搜索（O(N)）**，用**事件推送（0空闲Token）**替代**轮询（大量空查询）**。

    - "Max De Marzi's Blog (建模技巧)"1.2 从零构建1000文档知识图谱（Implementation）# neo4j_knowledge_graph_builder.py
# 针对1000文档规模的优化实现

from neo4j import GraphDatabase
from typing import List, Dict, Tuple
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime
import logging

class SatisfynowKnowledgeGraph:
    """
    满意妞专用知识图谱构建器
    优化目标：1000文档，<5分钟全量导入，查询延迟<100ms
    """
    
    def __init__(self, uri: str, user: str, password: str):
        self.driver = GraphDatabase.driver(uri, auth=(user, password))
        self.batch_size = 100  # 批量插入大小，优化性能
        self.setup_logging()
        
    def setup_logging(self):
        logging.basicConfig(
            level=logging.INFO,
            format='%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'
        )
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    
    def init_schema(self):
        """初始化图模式：约束、索引、全文搜索"""
        with self.driver.session() as session:
            # 唯一约束（防止重复）
            constraints = [
                "CREATE CONSTRAINT expert_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Expert) REQUIRE e.id IS UNIQUE",
                "CREATE CONSTRAINT doc_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (d:Document) REQUIRE d.id IS UNIQUE",
                "CREATE CONSTRAINT concept_name IF NOT EXISTS FOR (c:Concept) REQUIRE c.name IS UNIQUE",
                "CREATE CONSTRAINT tag_name IF NOT EXISTS FOR (t:Tag) REQUIRE t.name IS UNIQUE"
            ]
            
            for constraint in constraints:
                try:
                    session.run(constraint)
                    self.logger.info(f"Created constraint: {constraint[:50]}...")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.warning(f"Constraint may exist: {e}")
            
            # 性能索引
            indexes = [
                "CREATE INDEX expert_field IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Expert) ON (e.field)",
                "CREATE INDEX doc_date IF NOT EXISTS FOR (d:Document) ON (d.created_at)",
                "CREATE INDEX rel_confidence IF NOT EXISTS FOR ()-[r:MENTIONS]-() ON (r.confidence)"
            ]
            
            for index in indexes:
                session.run(index)
            
            # 全文索引（支持模糊搜索）
            session.run("""
                CALL db.index.fulltext.createNodeIndex('contentSearch', 
                    ['Document', 'Concept', 'Expert'], 
                    ['title', 'content', 'name', 'bio'])
            """)
            
            self.logger.info("Schema initialization complete")
    
    def extract_entities_lightweight(self, text: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        轻量级实体抽取（无外部API，节省Token）
        基于规则 + 本地词典（针对满意妞领域定制）
        """
        entities = []
        
        # 预定义词典（可扩展）
        expert_patterns = ["黎红雷", "方翊沣", "谢宝剑", "罗汉", "陈国祥", "XU先生"]
        field_patterns = ["国学管理", "阳明心学", "技术就绪度", "TRL", "商业计划"]
        org_patterns = ["满意解研究所", "清华大学", "中山大学"]
        
        # 简单匹配（实际可用更复杂的正则或本地NER模型）
        for pattern in expert_patterns:
            if pattern in text:
                entities.append({
                    "name": pattern,
                    "type": "Expert",
                    "confidence": 0.9,
                    "source": "dictionary"
                })
        
        for pattern in field_patterns:
            if pattern in text:
                entities.append({
                    "name": pattern,
                    "type": "Concept",
                    "confidence": 0.85,
                    "source": "dictionary"
                })
        
        return entities
    
    def ingest_document_batch(self, documents: List[Dict]):
        """
        批量文档入库（优化性能，支持1000文档）
        使用UNWIND批量操作，比单条快10倍
        """
        with self.driver.session() as session:
            # 分批处理
            for i in range(0, len(documents), self.batch_size):
                batch = documents[i:i + self.batch_size]
                
                cypher = """
                UNWIND $docs AS doc
                // 创建文档节点
                MERGE (d:Document {id: doc.id})
                SET d.title = doc.title,
                    d.content = doc.content,
                    d.content_hash = doc.hash,
                    d.source = doc.source,
                    d.created_at = datetime(doc.created_at),
                    d.status = doc.status
                
                // 提取并创建实体（简化版，实际可调用Python函数）
                WITH d, doc
                UNWIND doc.entities AS entity
                MERGE (e:Entity {name: entity.name, type: entity.type})
                ON CREATE SET e.first_seen = datetime()
                MERGE (d)-[r:MENTIONS {
                    confidence: entity.confidence,
                    context: entity.context,
                    timestamp: datetime()
                }]->(e)
                
                // 创建文档间关系（基于内容相似度）
                WITH d
                MATCH (other:Document)
                WHERE other <> d AND other.content_hash IN $related_hashes
                MERGE (d)-[:RELATED_TO {
                    similarity: 0.8,
                    basis: 'content_overlap'
                }]->(other)
                """
                
                try:
                    session.run(cypher, docs=batch, related_hashes=[])
                    self.logger.info(f"Inserted batch {i//self.batch_size + 1}/{(len(documents)-1)//self.batch_size + 1}")
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(f"Batch insertion failed: {e}")
                    raise
    
    def build_knowledge_network(self):
        """
        构建知识网络：专家-概念-文档关联
        使用GDS库进行图算法增强
        """
        with self.driver.session() as session:
            # 创建专家-概念关联（基于文档共现）
            session.run("""
                MATCH (e:Expert)<-[:MENTIONS]-(d:Document)-[:MENTIONS]->(c:Concept)
                WITH e, c, count(d) as strength
                WHERE strength >= 2  // 至少共同出现2次
                MERGE (e)-[:HAS_EXPERTISE {
                    level: CASE 
                        WHEN strength >= 5 THEN '精通'
                        WHEN strength >= 3 THEN '熟悉'
                        ELSE '了解'
                    END,
                    evidence_count: strength
                }]->(c)
            """)
            
            # 创建专家间合作网络（基于文档共作者）
            session.run("""
                MATCH (e1:Expert)<-[:MENTIONS]-(d:Document)-[:MENTIONS]->(e2:Expert)
                WHERE id(e1) < id(e2)  // 避免重复
                WITH e1, e2, count(d) as collaborations
                MERGE (e1)-[:COLLABORATED_WITH {
                    frequency: collaborations,
                    last_active: max(d.created_at)
                }]->(e2)
            """)
            
            self.logger.info("Knowledge network built")
    
    def query_expertise_graph(self, expert_name: str, depth: int = 2) -> Dict:
        """
        查询专家知识图谱（Claw快速获取上下文）
        优化：限制深度，避免遍历爆炸
        """
        with self.driver.session() as session:
            result = session.run("""
                MATCH path = (e:Expert {name: $name})-[*1..$depth]-(connected)
                WHERE connected:Expert OR connected:Concept OR connected:Document
                RETURN 
                    [node in nodes(path) | {name: node.name, type: labels(node)[0]}] as nodes,
                    [rel in relationships(path) | type(rel)] as relations,
                    length(path) as distance
                ORDER BY distance
                LIMIT 50
            """, name=expert_name, depth=depth)
            
            return [record.data() for record in result]
    
    def detect_knowledge_conflicts(self) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        蓝军审计：检测知识冲突
        例如：同一概念在不同文档中定义矛盾
        """
        with self.driver.session() as session:
            return session.run("""
                MATCH (c:Concept)<-[:DEFINES]-(d1:Document),
                      (c)<-[:DEFINES]-(d2:Document)
                WHERE id(d1) < id(d2) AND d1.definition <> d2.definition
                RETURN c.name as concept,
                       d1.title as doc1, d1.definition as def1,
                       d2.title as doc2, d2.definition as def2,
                       'definition_conflict' as conflict_type
            """).data()
    
    def close(self):
        self.driver.close()

# 使用示例（Claw执行）
if __name__ == "__main__":
    kg = SatisfynowKnowledgeGraph("bolt://localhost:7687", "neo4j", "password")
    
    # 1. 初始化
    kg.init_schema()
    
    # 2. 准备1000文档数据（示例）
    docs = [
        {
            "id": f"doc_{i:04d}",
            "title": f"Document {i}",
            "content": f"Content mentioning 黎红雷 and 阳明心学...",
            "hash": hashlib.md5(f"content{i}".encode()).hexdigest(),
            "source": "interview",
            "created_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "status": "active",
            "entities": [
                {"name": "黎红雷", "type": "Expert", "confidence": 0.9, "context": "提及专家"},
                {"name": "阳明心学", "type": "Concept", "confidence": 0.85, "context": "理论框架"}
            ]
        }
        for i in range(1000)
    ]
    
    # 3. 批量导入（1000文档约2-3分钟）
    kg.ingest_document_batch(docs)
    
    # 4. 构建网络
    kg.build_knowledge_network()
    
    # 5. 查询示例
    network = kg.query_expertise_graph("黎红雷", depth=2)
    print(f"Found {len(network)} connections")
    
    kg.close()1.3 Docker Compose部署（一键启动）# docker-compose.neo4j.yml

    - "Circuit Breaker (熔断)"2.2 轻量级事件总线实现（Redis Streams）# lightweight_event_bus.py
# 基于Redis Streams的轻量级事件总线
# 支持10个核心系统，零外部依赖（Redis已存在）

import redis
import json
import threading
import time
from typing import Callable, Dict, List
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
from enum import Enum
import logging

class EventPriority(Enum):
    CRITICAL = 1    # 立即处理（Blue Army警报）
    HIGH = 2        # 优先处理（用户指令）
    NORMAL = 3      # 普通处理（Cron任务）
    LOW = 4         # 延迟处理（日志聚合）

@dataclass
class Event:
    event_id: str
    event_type: str
    source: str          # 哪个系统产生的
    target: str          # 目标系统（或*表示广播）
    payload: Dict
    priority: EventPriority
    timestamp: float
    correlation_id: str  # 用于追踪分布式事务
    
    def to_json(self):
        return json.dumps(asdict(self), default=lambda x: x.value if isinstance(x, Enum) else x)
    
    @classmethod
    def from_json(cls, data):
        d = json.loads(data)
        d['priority'] = EventPriority(d['priority'])
        return cls(**d)

class SatisfynowEventBus:
    """
    满意妞轻量级事件总线
    基于Redis Streams，支持10个核心系统
    特性：优先级队列、死信队列、事件溯源
    """
    
    def __init__(self, redis_host='localhost', redis_port=6379):
        self.redis_client = redis.Redis(host=redis_host, port=redis_port, decode_responses=True)
        self.subscribers: Dict[str, List[Callable]] = {}
        self.running = False
        self.consumer_group = "satisfynow_consumers"
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
        
    def create_stream(self, stream_name: str):
        """创建事件流（如果不存在）"""
        try:
            self.redis_client.xgroup_create(stream_name, self.consumer_group, id='0', mkstream=True)
        except redis.ResponseError:
            pass  # 组已存在
    
    def publish(self, event: Event, stream: str = "satisfynow_events") -> str:
        """
        发布事件
        返回：消息ID（用于追踪）
        """
        # 根据优先级选择Stream（物理隔离）
        priority_streams = {
            EventPriority.CRITICAL: "satisfynow_critical",
            EventPriority.HIGH: "satisfynow_high",
            EventPriority.NORMAL: "satisfynow_normal",
            EventPriority.LOW: "satisfynow_low"
        }
        
        target_stream = priority_streams.get(event.priority, stream)
        
        event_id = self.redis_client.xadd(
            target_stream,
            {"event": event.to_json()},
            id='*'  # 自动ID
        )
        
        self.logger.info(f"Published {event.event_type} to {target_stream}: {event_id}")
        return event_id
    
    def subscribe(self, event_type: str, handler: Callable[[Event], None]):
        """订阅特定事件类型"""
        if event_type not in self.subscribers:
            self.subscribers[event_type] = []
        self.subscribers[event_type].append(handler)
        self.logger.info(f"Subscribed handler to {event_type}")
    
    def start_consumer(self, stream_patterns: List[str] = ["satisfynow_*"]):
        """
        启动事件消费者（阻塞监听）
        支持通配符匹配多个Stream
        """
        self.running = True
        self.logger.info("Event bus consumer started")
        
        # 创建消费者组
        for pattern in stream_patterns:
            for key in self.redis_client.keys(pattern):
                self.create_stream(key)
        
        while self.running:
            try:
                # 监听多个Stream（按优先级顺序）
                streams = {
                    "satisfynow_critical": ">",  # 新消息
                    "satisfynow_high": ">",
                    "satisfynow_normal": ">",
                    "satisfynow_low": ">"
                }
                
                # 阻塞读取（0表示无限阻塞，1000表示1秒超时）
                messages = self.redis_client.xreadgroup(
                    self.consumer_group,
                    "consumer_1",
                    streams,
                    count=10,  # 每次最多10条
                    block=1000
                )
                
                for stream_name, stream_messages in messages:
                    for msg_id, fields in stream_messages:
                        try:
                            event = Event.from_json(fields['event'])
                            self._process_event(event, msg_id, stream_name)
                        except Exception as e:
                            self._handle_failed_event(event, msg_id, stream_name, e)
                            
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Consumer error: {e}")
                time.sleep(1)
    
    def _process_event(self, event: Event, msg_id: str, stream: str):
        """处理事件"""
        handlers = self.subscribers.get(event.event_type, [])
        
        if not handlers:
            self.logger.warning(f"No handlers for {event.event_type}")
            return
        
        for handler in handlers:
            try:
                handler(event)
                # 确认消息已处理（ACK）
                self.redis_client.xack(stream, self.consumer_group, msg_id)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Handler failed for {event.event_type}: {e}")
                raise  # 触发死信队列逻辑
    
    def _handle_failed_event(self, event: Event, msg_id: str, stream: str, error: Exception):
        """失败事件处理：重试3次，然后转入死信队列"""
        retry_key = f"retry:{msg_id}"
        retry_count = self.redis_client.incr(retry_key)
        
        if retry_count <= 3:
            # 延迟重试（指数退避）
            time.sleep(2 ** retry_count)
            # 重新发布（不ACK，让其他消费者重试）
        else:
            # 转入死信队列
            dead_event = {
                "original_event": event.to_json(),
                "error": str(error),
                "retry_count": retry_count,
                "failed_at": time.time()
            }
            self.redis_client.xadd("satisfynow_dead_letter", dead_event)
            self.redis_client.xack(stream, self.consumer_group, msg_id)
            self.redis_client.delete(retry_key)
            self.logger.error(f"Event moved to dead letter: {msg_id}")

# 集成示例：Claw系统间通信
class ClawSystemIntegration:
    def __init__(self, event_bus: SatisfynowEventBus):
        self.bus = event_bus
        self.setup_handlers()
    
    def setup_handlers(self):
        """设置10个核心系统的事件处理器"""
        
        # 系统1: Blue Sentinel（蓝军审计）
        self.bus.subscribe("knowledge_updated", self.blue_sentinel_audit)
        
        # 系统2: Token Budget Enforcer
        self.bus.subscribe("token_consumed", self.token_monitor)
        
        # 系统3: Cron Scheduler
        self.bus.subscribe("cron_triggered", self.task_executor)
        
        # 系统4: Knowledge Graph Updater
        self.bus.subscribe("document_ingested", self.graph_updater)
        
        # 系统5: Notification Dispatcher
        self.bus.subscribe("alert_generated", self.notify_user)
        
        # 系统6: Backup Manager
        self.bus.subscribe("checkpoint_reached", self.backup_trigger)
        
        # 系统7: Quality Gate
        self.bus.subscribe("skill_deployed", self.quality_check)
        
        # 系统8: Meta Cognitive Evolver
        self.bus.subscribe("pattern_detected", self.evolution_trigger)
        
        # 系统9: User Interaction Logger
        self.bus.subscribe("user_command", self.interaction_logger)
        
        # 系统10: External API Sync
        self.bus.subscribe("api_response", self.external_sync)
    
    def blue_sentinel_audit(self, event: Event):
        """蓝军自动审计新入库知识"""
        if event.event_type == "knowledge_updated":
            # 触发矛盾检测
            conflicts = detect_conflicts(event.payload)
            if conflicts:
                self.bus.publish(Event(
                    event_id=f"alert_{time.time()}",
                    event_type="knowledge_conflict_detected",
                    source="blue_sentinel",
                    target="user",
                    payload={"conflicts": conflicts},
                    priority=EventPriority.HIGH,
                    timestamp=time.time(),
                    correlation_id=event.correlation_id
                ))

# 轻量级YAML工作流引擎，支持状态机、依赖、错误处理

import yaml
import json
import time
import threading
from enum import Enum, auto
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Dict, List, Any, Callable, Optional
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
import logging

class TaskStatus(Enum):
    PENDING = auto()
    RUNNING = auto()
    SUCCESS = auto()
    FAILED = auto()
    RETRYING = auto()
    SKIPPED = auto()

@dataclass
class Task:
    id: str
    name: str
    type: str  # "python", "shell", "http", "decision"
    config: Dict[str, Any]
    depends_on: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    retries: int = 3
    timeout: int = 300  # 秒
    condition: Optional[str] = None  # 执行条件（Python表达式）
    
    # 运行时状态
    status: TaskStatus = TaskStatus.PENDING
    output: Any = None
    error: Optional[str] = None
    start_time: Optional[float] = None
    end_time: Optional[float] = None

class WorkflowEngine:
    """
    满意妞工作流引擎
    特性：YAML定义、DAG依赖解析、自动重试、状态持久化
    """
    
    def __init__(self, max_workers=5):
        self.tasks: Dict[str, Task] = {}
        self.adapters: Dict[str, Callable] = {
            "python": self._execute_python,
            "shell": self._execute_shell,
            "http": self._execute_http,
            "decision": self._execute_decision,
            "neo4j_query": self._execute_neo4j,
            "event_publish": self._execute_event_publish
        }
        self.executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers)
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
        self.state_persistence = {}  # 可替换为Redis/数据库
    
    def load_workflow(self, yaml_file: str) -> List[Task]:
        """从YAML加载工作流定义"""
        with open(yaml_file, 'r') as f:
            workflow_def = yaml.safe_load(f)
        
        tasks = []
        for task_def in workflow_def['tasks']:
            task = Task(
                id=task_def['id'],
                name=task_def['name'],
                type=task_def['type'],
                config=task_def.get('config', {}),
                depends_on=task_def.get('depends_on', []),
                retries=task_def.get('retries', 3),
                timeout=task_def.get('timeout', 300),
                condition=task_def.get('condition')
            )
            tasks.append(task)
            self.tasks[task.id] = task
        
        # 验证DAG无环
        self._validate_dag()
        return tasks
    
    def _validate_dag(self):
        """验证依赖图无环（拓扑排序检查）"""
        visited = set()
        temp_mark = set()
        
        def dfs(task_id):
            if task_id in temp_mark:
                raise ValueError(f"Circular dependency detected at {task_id}")
            if task_id in visited:
                return
            
            temp_mark.add(task_id)
            for dep_id in self.tasks[task_id].depends_on:
                dfs(dep_id)
            temp_mark.remove(task_id)
            visited.add(task_id)
        
        for task_id in self.tasks:
            dfs(task_id)
        
        self.logger.info("DAG validation passed")
    
    def execute_workflow(self, context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        执行工作流
        支持并行执行无依赖任务
        """
        context = context or {}
        completed = set()
        failed = set()
        
        while len(completed) + len(failed) < len(self.tasks):
            # 找出可执行的任务（依赖已完成）
            ready_tasks = [
                t for t in self.tasks.values()
                if t.status == TaskStatus.PENDING
                and all(d in completed for d in t.depends_on)
                and not any(d in failed for d in t.depends_on)  # 依赖失败则跳过
            ]
            
            if not ready_tasks and len(completed) + len(failed) < len(self.tasks):
                # 死锁检测
                raise RuntimeError("Workflow deadlock detected")
            
            # 提交并行执行
            futures = {
                self.executor.submit(self._execute_task, task, context): task
                for task in ready_tasks
            }
            
            # 等待完成
            for future in as_completed(futures):
                task = futures[future]
                try:
                    result = future.result()
                    task.status = TaskStatus.SUCCESS
                    task.output = result
                    completed.add(task.id)
                    context[f"{task.id}_output"] = result  # 供后续任务使用
                except Exception as e:
                    task.status = TaskStatus.FAILED
                    task.error = str(e)
                    failed.add(task.id)
                    self.logger.error(f"Task {task.id} failed: {e}")
        
        return {
            "status": "SUCCESS" if not failed else "PARTIAL" if completed else "FAILED",
            "completed": list(completed),
            "failed": list(failed),
            "outputs": {tid: t.output for tid, t in self.tasks.items() if t.output}
        }
    
    def _execute_task(self, task: Task, context: Dict) -> Any:
        """执行单个任务（带重试和超时）"""
        # 条件判断
        if task.condition:
            if not eval(task.condition, {"__builtins__": {}}, context):
                task.status = TaskStatus.SKIPPED
                return None
        
        task.status = TaskStatus.RUNNING
        task.start_time = time.time()
        
        executor = self.adapters.get(task.type)
        if not executor:
            raise ValueError(f"Unknown task type: {task.type}")
        
        # 重试逻辑
        for attempt in range(task.retries):
            try:
                # 实际执行（带超时）
                result = executor(task.config, context)
                task.end_time = time.time()
                return result
            except Exception as e:
                if attempt < task.retries - 1:
                    task.status = TaskStatus.RETRYING
                    time.sleep(2 ** attempt)  # 指数退避
                else:
                    raise e
    
    def _execute_python(self, config: Dict, context: Dict) -> Any:
        """执行Python代码（安全沙箱）"""
        code = config.get('code')
        # 注意：实际生产应使用RestrictedPython等沙箱
        local_vars = context.copy()
        exec(code, {"__builtins__": {}}, local_vars)
        return local_vars.get('result')
    
    def _execute_shell(self, config: Dict, context: Dict) -> str:
        import subprocess
        result = subprocess.run(
            config['command'],
            shell=True,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=config.get('timeout', 60)
        )
        if result.returncode != 0:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Shell command failed: {result.stderr}")
        return result.stdout
    
    def _execute_http(self, config: Dict, context: Dict) -> Dict:
        import requests
        response = requests.request(
            method=config.get('method', 'GET'),
            url=config['url'],
            json=config.get('body'),
            headers=config.get('headers'),
            timeout=config.get('timeout', 30)
        )
        response.raise_for_status()
        return response.json()
    
    def _execute_decision(self, config: Dict, context: Dict) -> str:
        """决策任务：根据条件选择分支"""
        for condition, result in config['branches'].items():
            if eval(condition, {"__builtins__": {}}, context):
                return result
        return config.get('default', 'default_branch')
    
    def _execute_neo4j(self, config: Dict, context: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """执行Neo4j查询"""
        from neo4j import GraphDatabase
        driver = GraphDatabase.driver(config['uri'], auth=(config['user'], config['password']))
        with driver.session() as session:
            result = session.run(config['query'], **context)
            return [record.data() for record in result]
    
    def _execute_event_publish(self, config: Dict, context: Dict) -> str:
        """发布事件到事件总线"""
        # 与前面的EventBus集成
        from lightweight_event_bus import SatisfynowEventBus, Event, EventPriority
        bus = SatisfynowEventBus()
        event = Event(
            event_id=f"wf_{time.time()}",
            event_type=config['event_type'],
            source="workflow_engine",
            target=config.get('target', '*'),
            payload=config.get('payload', {}),
            priority=EventPriority[config.get('priority', 'NORMAL')],
            timestamp=time.time(),
            correlation_id=context.get('correlation_id')
        )
        return bus.publish(event)

# YAML工作流示例（与Claw现有系统集成）
"""
# workflow_knowledge_ingestion.yaml
name: 知识入库工作流
description: 自动处理文档入库、实体抽取、图谱更新、通知发送

tasks:
  - id: validate_document
    name: 验证文档格式
    type: python
    config:
      code: |
        import hashlib
        result = hashlib.md5(context['content'].encode()).hexdigest()
    retries: 1

  - id: extract_entities
    name: 实体抽取
    type: python
    depends_on: [validate_document]
    config:
      code: |
        # 使用本地NER模型
        entities = [{"name": "黎红雷", "type": "Expert"}]
        result = entities

  - id: check_conflicts
    name: 蓝军冲突检测
    type: neo4j_query
    depends_on: [extract_entities]
    config:
      uri: bolt://localhost:7687
      user: neo4j
      password: password
      query: |
        MATCH (c:Concept {name: $concept_name})<-[:DEFINES]-(d:Document)
        RETURN count(d) as existing_defs
    # 如果已存在定义，走更新分支，否则走创建分支

  - id: update_graph
    name: 更新知识图谱
    type: neo4j_query
    depends_on: [check_conflicts]
    condition: "check_conflicts_output[0]['existing_defs'] > 0"
    config:
      query: |
        MERGE (d:Document {id: $doc_id})
        SET d.content = $content

  - id: notify_user
    name: 通知用户完成
    type: event_publish
    depends_on: [update_graph]
    config:
      event_type: "knowledge_ingested"
      target: "user"
      priority: "NORMAL"
      payload:
        message: "文档已入库"
        doc_id: "{{doc_id}}"
"""需求4：可观测性平台（轻量级）4.1 学习资源地图learning_resources:

      desc: "通过API管理Dashboard"4.2 轻量级可观测性平台实现# satisfynow_observability.py
# 集成Metrics + Logs + Dashboard + Alerting

from prometheus_client import Counter, Gauge, Histogram, start_http_server, CollectorRegistry
import logging
import json
import time
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, Any, List
import threading
import requests

class SatisfynowObservability:
    """
    满意妞可观测性平台
    轻量级实现：Prometheus Metrics + JSON日志 + 简易Dashboard
    """
    
    def __init__(self, service_name: str = "claw"):
        self.service = service_name
        self.registry = CollectorRegistry()
        
        # Metrics定义（RED方法）
        self.request_count = Counter(
            'claw_requests_total',
            'Total requests',
            ['method', 'endpoint', 'status'],
            registry=self.registry
        )
        
        self.request_duration = Histogram(
            'claw_request_duration_seconds',
            'Request latency',
            ['method', 'endpoint'],
            buckets=[0.01, 0.05, 0.1, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 5.0],
            registry=self.registry
        )
        
        self.token_consumption = Counter(
            'claw_token_consumed_total',
            'Token consumed by operation',
            ['operation', 'model'],
            registry=self.registry
        )
        
        self.active_tasks = Gauge(
            'claw_active_tasks',
            'Current active tasks',
            ['task_type'],
            registry=self.registry
        )
        
        self.knowledge_graph_stats = Gauge(
            'claw_kg_stats',
            'Knowledge graph statistics',
            ['metric'],  # nodes, relationships, density
            registry=self.registry
        )
        
        # 日志设置（结构化JSON）
        self.logger = self._setup_logger()
        
        # Dashboard数据（内存存储，可持久化到Redis）
        self.metrics_history: List[Dict] = []
        self.max_history = 1000  # 保留最近1000个数据点
    
    def _setup_logger(self):
        """配置结构化日志"""
        logger = logging.getLogger("claw_observability")
        logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
        
        handler = logging.StreamHandler()
        formatter = logging.Formatter(
            '%(asctime)s - %(name)s - %(levelname)s - %(message)s'
        )
        handler.setFormatter(formatter)
        logger.addHandler(handler)
        
        return logger
    
    def record_request(self, method: str, endpoint: str, status: int, duration: float):
        """记录HTTP/API请求（RED方法）"""
        self.request_count.labels(method=method, endpoint=endpoint, status=status).inc()
        self.request_duration.labels(method=method, endpoint=endpoint).observe(duration)
        
        # 结构化日志
        self.logger.info(json.dumps({
            "event": "http_request",
            "method": method,
            "endpoint": endpoint,
            "status": status,
            "duration": duration,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }))
    
    def record_token_usage(self, operation: str, model: str, tokens: int):
        """记录Token消耗（关键指标）"""
        self.token_consumption.labels(operation=operation, model=model).inc(tokens)
        
        # 告警检查（超过阈值）
        if tokens > 1000:
            self.trigger_alert("HIGH_TOKEN_USAGE", {
                "operation": operation,
                "tokens": tokens,
                "threshold": 1000
            })
    
    def update_kg_metrics(self, nodes: int, relationships: int):
        """更新知识图谱指标"""
        self.knowledge_graph_stats.labels(metric="nodes").set(nodes)
        self.knowledge_graph_stats.labels(metric="relationships").set(relationships)
        density = relationships / max(nodes, 1)
        self.knowledge_graph_stats.labels(metric="density").set(density)
    
    def trigger_alert(self, alert_type: str, payload: Dict):
        """触发告警（可集成到企微/飞书）"""
        alert = {
            "type": alert_type,
            "severity": "WARNING" if "HIGH" in alert_type else "INFO",
            "payload": payload,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "service": self.service
        }
        
        self.logger.warning(json.dumps(alert))
        
        # 发送到事件总线（与前面EDA集成）
        from lightweight_event_bus import SatisfynowEventBus, Event, EventPriority
        bus = SatisfynowEventBus()
        bus.publish(Event(
            event_id=f"alert_{time.time()}",
            event_type="system_alert",
            source="observability",
            target="notification_service",
            payload=alert,
            priority=EventPriority.HIGH if alert["severity"] == "WARNING" else EventPriority.NORMAL,
            timestamp=time.time(),
            correlation_id=""
        ))
    
    def generate_dashboard_html(self) -> str:
        """
        生成简易Dashboard HTML（无需Grafana）
        可在浏览器中打开查看实时状态
        """
        # 收集当前指标
        metrics = {
            "requests_total": self.request_count._value.get(),
            "token_consumed": self.token_consumption._value.get(),
            "active_tasks": {k: v for k, v in self.active_tasks._value.items()},
            "kg_nodes": self.knowledge_graph_stats.labels(metric="nodes")._value.get(),
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
        
        self.metrics_history.append(metrics)
        if len(self.metrics_history) > self.max_history:
            self.metrics_history.pop(0)
        
        # 生成HTML（简化版，实际可用Chart.js美化）
        html = f"""
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html>
        <head>
            <title>Satisfynow Claw Dashboard</title>
            <meta http-equiv="refresh" content="30">
            <style>
                body {{ font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 20px; }}
                .metric {{ background: #f0f0f0; padding: 15px; margin: 10px 0; border-radius: 5px; }}
                .alert {{ background: #ffcccc; }}
                .healthy {{ background: #ccffcc; }}
                table {{ border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; }}
                th, td {{ border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px; text-align: left; }}
            </style>
        </head>
        <body>
            <h1>🦞 Satisfynow Claw 系统健康度</h1>
            <p>最后更新: {metrics['timestamp']}</p>
            
            <div class="metric {'alert' if metrics['token_consumed'] > 10000 else 'healthy'}">
                <h3>Token消耗</h3>
                <p>总计: {metrics['token_consumed']}</p>
                <p>状态: {'⚠️ 接近限额' if metrics['token_consumed'] > 10000 else '✅ 正常'}</p>
            </div>
            
            <div class="metric">
                <h3>知识图谱</h3>
                <p>节点数: {metrics['kg_nodes']}</p>
                <p>目标: 1000 | 完成度: {metrics['kg_nodes']/10:.1f}%</p>
            </div>
            
            <div class="metric">
                <h3>活跃任务</h3>
                <table>
                    <tr><th>任务类型</th><th>数量</th></tr>
                    {''.join(f"<tr><td>{k}</td><td>{v}</td></tr>" for k, v in metrics['active_tasks'].items())}
                </table>
            </div>
            
            <h2>最近事件</h2>
            <pre>{json.dumps(self.metrics_history[-10:], indent=2)}</pre>
        </body>
        </html>
        """
        
        # 保存到文件（可通过HTTP服务访问）
        with open('/tmp/claw_dashboard.html', 'w') as f:
            f.write(html)
        
        return html
    
    def start_metrics_server(self, port=8000):
        """启动Prometheus指标服务器"""
        start_http_server(port, registry=self.registry)
        self.logger.info(f"Metrics server started on port {port}")
        
        # 后台线程定期生成Dashboard
        def dashboard_updater():
            while True:
                self.generate_dashboard_html()
                time.sleep(30)  # 30秒更新一次
        
        threading.Thread(target=dashboard_updater, daemon=True).start()

# 使用示例（与Claw现有系统集成）
obs = SatisfynowObservability("claw_core")

# 启动指标服务器（后台）
obs.start_metrics_server(8000)

# 在Skill中使用装饰器自动记录
def monitored_skill(func):
    """Skill监控装饰器"""
    def wrapper(*args, **kwargs):
        start = time.time()
        obs.active_tasks.labels(task_type=func.__name__).inc()
        
        try:
            result = func(*args, **kwargs)
            status = "success"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            status = "error"
            obs.logger.error(f"Skill {func.__name__} failed: {e}")
            raise
        finally:
            duration = time.time() - start
            obs.record_request("skill", func.__name__, 200 if status == "success" else 500, duration)
            obs.active_tasks.labels(task_type=func.__name__).dec()
    
    return wrapper

# 应用到现有Skill
@monitored_skill
def knowledge_ingestion_skill(doc_content):
    # 原逻辑...
    obs.record_token_usage("ingestion", "kimi", 500)  # 记录Token消耗
    obs.update_kg_metrics(nodes=1051, relationships=5000)  # 更新图谱指标
    pass五、四位一体集成方案# 四位系统协同架构

integration_architecture:
  data_flow: |
    1. 工作流引擎(Workflow)触发任务
       ↓
    2. 任务执行产生Metrics/Observability数据
       ↓
    3. 关键事件发布到Event Bus
       ↓
    4. Event Bus分发到：
       - Neo4j（知识更新）
       - Blue Army（审计）
       - Notification（告警）
       - Workflow（触发下游任务）
       ↓
    5. 所有数据持久化到各自存储，并在Dashboard可视化

  shared_services:
    - "Redis: 事件总线 + 工作流状态 + 缓存"
    - "Neo4j: 知识图谱 + 工作流依赖图"
    - "Prometheus: 指标收集"
    - "FileSystem: 日志 + Dashboard HTML"

  deployment:
    docker_compose: |
      version: '3.8'
      services:
        redis:  # 事件总线 + 工作流状态
          image: redis:7-alpine
          ports: ["6379:6379"]
        
        neo4j:  # 知识图谱
          image: neo4j:5.15-enterprise
          ports: ["7474:7474", "7687:7687"]
          environment:
            - NEO4J_AUTH=neo4j/password
        
        claw_core:  # 主应用
          build: .
          volumes:
            - ./workflows:/app/workflows
            - ./logs:/app/logs
          depends_on:
            - redis
            - neo4j
          ports: ["8000:8000"]  # Prometheus + Dashboard
        
        # 可选：Grafana用于高级Dashboard
        grafana:
          image: grafana/grafana:latest
          ports: ["3000:3000"]
          volumes:
            - ./grafana/dashboards:/etc/grafana/provisioning/dashboards六、给Claw的执行指令（14天计划）[系统指令 - 四位一体技术栈建设]
